It tracks where I go... they're just trying to get rid of the older guys.\n\nDoctor: Do you think anyone else is involved in trying to push you out of your job, or is it just the routing system?\n\nPatient: I don't know names. I just... like I said, it's the system. Whoever runs it. It's not a normal computer anymore. The scanners are what's sending the messages and marking my moves. They just want the old hands gone. I don't really want to guess who.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard the routing system or scanners sending you messages through sound, like a voice?\n\nPatient: I... yeah. Sometimes. It's like dispatch voices. Even if I'm just sitting in my garage at home... everything shut off. I still hear them clear as day. Calling my operator number... saying I'm dangerous. That security is coming for me.\n\nDoctor: Do you still hear the dispatch voices when you're not thinking about work or the routing system?\n\nPatient: Yeah... like I just said. It doesn't really matter what's on my mind. \n\nI could just be out in my garage... everything shut down, not thinking about the warehouse at all. And I still hear them. Calling my operator number... telling me I'm dangerous and that security is on the way. So... that's why I'm here.\n\nDoctor: Do you really believe the routing system is intentionally trying to push you out of your job?\n\nPatient: Yeah. I... I wouldn't have said it if I didn't know it was true. Like I just told you, it's not just a regular machine to me anymore. I see what comes through the scanners. It tracks where I go... they're just trying to get rid of the older guys.\n\nDoctor: Do you think anyone else is involved in trying to push you out of your job, or is it just the routing system?\n\nPatient: I don't know names. I just... like I said, it's the system. Whoever runs it. It's not a normal computer anymore. The scanners are what's sending the messages and marking my moves. They just want the old hands gone. I don't really want to guess who.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard the routing system or scanners sending you messages through sound, like a voice?\n\nPatient: I... yeah. Sometimes. It's like dispatch voices. Even if I'm just sitting in my garage at home... everything shut off. I still hear them clear as day. Calling my operator number... saying I'm dangerous. That security is coming for me.\n\nDoctor: Do you still hear the dispatch voices when you're not thinking about work or the routing system?\n\nPatient: Yeah... like I just said. It doesn't really matter what's on my mind. \n\nI could just be out in my garage... everything shut down, not thinking about the warehouse at all. And I still hear them. Calling my operator number... telling me I'm dangerous and that security is on the way. Doesn't matter if I'm thinking about work or not.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it hard to keep track of what you're saying when you're talking about the routing system and the voices you hear?\n\nPatient: No. I... I know what I'm saying. I just don't like talking about it much.\n\nDoctor: Do the messages from the routing system and the voices you hear seem to be part of a larger plan or story?\n\nPatient: ...Yeah. I guess you could say that. \n\nIt's... it's not just a computer down there anymore. I know it's been marking my moves. Sending those messages through the scanners. It's just... it's trying to push me out. Because I'm one of the old hands. That's what it's doing.\n\nDoctor: Would you say the idea of a computer routing system intentionally trying to push you out of your job is something that could happen in real life?\n\nPatient: ...It is real. It's happening. \n\nLike I just said, it's... it's not just a computer to me anymore. I know what it's doing. Marking my moves... sending those messages right through the scanners to push me out. Just because I'm one of the old hands. \n\nI wouldn't be sitting here talking to you if it wasn't happening.\n\nDoctor: Would you say the idea of a computer routing system being able to think and act on its own to push you out of your job is a strange or unusual one?\n\nPatient: ...No. I don't see what's strange about it. \n\nI'm... I don't know what else to tell you.\n\nDoctor: Have your thoughts about the routing system trying to push you out of your job ever changed or wavered since you first started believing it?\n\nPatient: ...No. It hasn't changed. \n\nI mean... I keep telling you. It's why I'm here. That system... it's not just a machine anymore. I know it's marking my moves. Sending those messages to the scanners. It wants me out because I've been there too long. \n\nThat's just... that's just how it is.
